   Local startup reproducibility: PARTIAL ✓ (with caveats)                    │                                                                               │    The compose.yaml structure supports reproducible startup:                  │                                                                               │    - Services start in correct order (db → api → web)                         │    - Healthchecks ensure readiness before dependents start                    │    - Port mappings and volume mounts are explicit                             │
   The compose.yaml structure supports reproducible startup:                  │                                                                               │    - Services start in correct order (db → api → web)                         │    - Healthchecks ensure readiness before dependents start                    │    - Port mappings and volume mounts are explicit                             │                                                                               │    However, the build process is non-deterministic:
